match result {\n        Ok(body) => respond(stream, status, &body),\n        Err(PlayReplayError::Invalid) => respond(stream, 400, r#\"{\"error\":\"invalid request\"}\"#),\n        Err(PlayReplayError::UnknownCampaign) => respond(stream, 404, r#\"{\"error\":\"not found\"}\"#),\n        Err(PlayReplayError::Forbidden) => respond(stream, 403, r#\"{\"error\":\"forbidden\"}\"#),\n        Err(PlayReplayError::Conflict) => respond(stream, 409, r#\"{\"error\":\"conflict\"}\"#),\n        Err(PlayReplayError::Storage) => respond(stream, 500, r#\"{\"error\":\"storage failure\"}\"#),\n    }\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug)]\nenum PlayFeedError { Invalid, UnknownCampaign, Forbidden, Conflict, Storage }\n\nfn feed_event_json(event: &PlayFeedEvent) -> String {\n    format!(r#\"{{\"event_id\":\"{}\",\"text\":\"{}\",\"sequence\":{}}}\"#, escape_json(&event.event_id), escape_json(&event.text), event.sequence)\n}\n\nfn append_play_feed_event(campaign_id: &str, actor: &str, body: &str, storage: &Storage) -> Result<String, PlayFeedError> {\n    if !valid_campaign_text(campaign_id) { return Err(PlayFeedError::Invalid); }\n    let obj = object(body).map_err(|_| PlayFeedError::Invalid)?;\n    if obj.len() != 2 { return Err(PlayFeedError::Invalid); }\n    let event_id = string_field(&obj, \"event_id\").map_err(|_| PlayFeedError::Invalid)?;\n    let text = string_field(&obj, \"text\").map_err(|_| PlayFeedError::Invalid)?;\n    if event_id.is_empty() || text.is_empty() { return Err(PlayFeedError::Invalid); }\n    match storage.append_play_feed_event(campaign_id, actor, event_id, text).map_err(|_| PlayFeedError::Storage)? {\n        PlayFeedResult::Found(event) => Ok(feed_event_json(&event)),\n        PlayFeedResult::UnknownCampaign => Err(PlayFeedError::UnknownCampaign),\n        PlayFeedResult::Forbidden => Err(PlayFeedError::Forbidden),\n        PlayFeedResult::Conflict => Err(PlayFeedError::Conflict),\n    }\n}\n\nfn parse_feed_page(query: &str) -> Result<(i64, i64), PlayFeedError> {\n    let mut cursor = 0;\n    let mut limit = 2;\n    let mut seen_cursor = false;\n    let mut seen_limit = false;\n    if !query.is_empty() {\n        for part in query.split('&') {\n            let Some((name, value)) = part.split_once('=') else { return Err(PlayFeedError::Invalid); };\n            match name {\n                \"cursor\" if !seen_cursor => { cursor = value.parse::<i64>().map_err(|_| PlayFeedError::Invalid)?; seen_cursor = true; }\n                \"limit\" if !seen_limit => { limit = value.parse::<i64>().map_err(|_| PlayFeedError::Invalid)?; seen_limit = true; }\n                _ => return Err(PlayFeedError::Invalid),\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    if cursor < 0 || !(1..=3).contains(&limit) { return Err(PlayFeedError::Invalid); }\n    Ok((cursor, limit))\n}\n\nfn get_play_feed(campaign_id: &str, actor: &str, query: &str, storage: &Storage) -> Result<String, PlayFeedError> {\n    if !valid_campaign_text(campaign_id) { return Err(PlayFeedError::Invalid); }\n    let (cursor, limit) = parse_feed_page(query)?;\n    match storage.play_feed(campaign_id, actor, cursor, limit).map_err(|_| PlayFeedError::Storage)? {\n        PlayFeedResult::Found(events) => Ok(format!(r#\"{{\"events\":[{}],\"next_cursor\":{}}}\"#, events.iter().map(feed_event_json).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(\",\"), cursor + events.len() as i64)),\n        PlayFeedResult::UnknownCampaign => Err(PlayFeedError::UnknownCampaign),\n        PlayFeedResult::Forbidden => Err(PlayFeedError::Forbidden),\n        PlayFeedResult::Conflict => Err(PlayFeedError::Storage),\n    }\n}\n\nfn feed_response(result: Result<String, PlayFeedError>, stream: &mut TcpStream, status: u16) -> std::io::Result<()> {\n    match result {\n        Ok(body) => respond(stream, status, &body),\n        Err(PlayFeedError::Invalid) => respond(stream, 400, r#\"{\"error\":\"invalid request\"}\"#),\n        Err(PlayFeedError::UnknownCampaign) => respond(stream, 404, r#\"{\"error\":\"not found\"}\"#),\n        Err(PlayFeedError::Forbidden) => respond(stream, 403, r#\"{\"error\":\"forbidden\"}\"#),\n        Err(PlayFeedError::Conflict) => respond(stream, 409, r#\"{\"error\":\"conflict\"}\"#),\n        Err(PlayFeedError::Storage) => respond(stream, 500, r#\"{\"error\":\"storage failure\"}\"#),\n    }\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug)]\nenum PlayRngError { Invalid, MissingSeed, UnknownCampaign, Forbidden, Conflict, Storage }\n\nfn
